Q1. Pipe A fills a tank in 12 hours. A drain pipe can empty the tank in 36 hours. If both are open, what is the filling time?
A. 9 3/5 hours
B. 13 hours
C. 16 hours
D. 12 hours
Answer: 12 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{6}-\frac{1}{12}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
B=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 12 hours

---

Q2. Two workers take turns, with A starting. A handles 10 files per turn and B handles 5 files per turn. In how many days will 72 files be finished?
A. 11 3/4 days
B. 9 2/5 days
C. 7 2/5 days
D. 15 2/5 days
Answer: 9 2/5 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_terminal_overshoot_cycle
Explanation:
Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[
W_{cycle}=10+5
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=9.4
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 9 2/5 days

---

Q3. Three machines run together for the stated hours and make 20, 25 and 30 items each hour. Find the total number of items?
A. 367 items
B. 370 items
C. 365 items
D. 273.75 items
Answer: 365 items
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_machine_parallel_scheduling
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
O=\sum r_it_i
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
O=20\times 6+25\times 5+30\times 4
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
O=365
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
O=\sum r_it_i
\]
Answer: 365 items

---

Q4. The pairs A+B, B+C and C+A finish a job in 10, 12 and 15 days respectively. What is A's individual time?
A. 23 days
B. 29 days
C. 24 days
D. 18 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_pairwise_ab_bc_ac
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
2(A+B+C)=(A+B)+(B+C)+(C+A)
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
AB=10,\ BC=12,\ CA=15
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_A=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
A=\frac{AB+AC-BC}{2}
\]
Answer: 24 days

---

Q5. Pipe A takes 12 hours and pipe B takes 18 hours to fill the tank alone. If pipe B is opened 2 hours later, how many hours are needed from the start?
A. 12 hours
B. 6 hours
C. 4 hours
D. 8 hours
Answer: 8 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: pc_multiple_pipes_timing
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W_1=r_At_1,\quad T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=3\times 2
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=2+\frac{30}{3+2}=8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Answer: 8 hours

---

Q6. One worker can do a job in 15 days and another can do it in 30 days. How long will they take together?
A. 22 1/2 days
B. 10 days
C. 45 days
D. 15 days
Answer: 10 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(15,30)=30
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=30/15=2,\quad B=30/30=1
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{30}{2+1}=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{15\times 30}{15+30}=10
\]
Answer: 10 days

---

Q7. A contract payment of ₹9000 is shared among A, B and C in the ratio 8:12. What amount goes to A?
A. ₹3595
B. ₹3600
C. ₹4500
D. ₹3599
Answer: ₹3600
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_wage_partial_time
Explanation:
Convert each person's work into contribution units.
\[
Share=\frac{c_i}{\sum c}\times W
\]
Use contribution ratio for the share.
\[
Share=\frac{8}{20}\times 9000
\]
Apply the wage or contract amount.
\[
Share=3600
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
Share=contribution\ ratio\times wage
\]
Answer: ₹3600

---

Q8. A filling tap adds 12 litres in an hour, then an emptying tap removes 5 litres in the next hour. The tank holds 60 litres. When will the tank become full?
A. 16 1/3 hours
B. 12 1/3 hours
C. 24 1/2 hours
D. 14 1/3 hours
Answer: 16 1/3 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: pc_alternating_spill_cycle
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
W_{cycle}=12-5
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
60\ units,\ cycle=7
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
T=16\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=C_f+t_f
\]
Answer: 16 1/3 hours

---

Q9. A teacher evaluates 40 answer sheets in 8 days. What is the daily number of answer sheets evaluated?
A. 11 answer sheets
B. 5 answer sheets
C. 8 answer sheets
D. 6.25 answer sheets
Answer: 5 answer sheets

Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r=\frac{W}{t}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
r=\frac{40}{8}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
r=5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
r=W/t
\]
Answer: 5 answer sheets

Q10. 15 workers can complete a job in 16 days by working 6 hours a day. How many workers are needed to finish it in 12 days by working 8 hours a day?
A. 18 workers
B. 11 workers
C. 20 workers
D. 15 workers
Answer: 15 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_man_days_hours_basic
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
15\times 16\times 6=M_2\times 12\times 8
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
M_2=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{15\times 16\times 6}{12\times 8}=15
\]
Answer: 15 workers

---

Q11. A team can complete a job in 15 days. If it works for 2 days, what fraction of the job will still be unfinished?
A. 15/13
B. 2/15
C. 13/2
D. 13/15
Answer: 13/15
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_remaining_work_fraction
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
R=1-\frac{t}{T}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
R=1-\frac{2}{15}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
R=\frac{13}{15}=13/15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{15-2}{15}=13/15
\]
Answer: 13/15

---

Q12. A tank receives water for 30 minutes at 15 litres per minute. What is the quantity of water received?

\[

\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[

\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:

Q14. If wages for equal time are ₹800 and ₹1200 for A and B, what is their work ratio?
A. 2:3
B. 1200:800
C. 80:60
D. 2000:1200
Answer: 2:3
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_efficiency_from_wages
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
W_A:W_B=E_A:E_B
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
E_A:E_B=800:1200
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
E_A:E_B=2:3
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
E=2:3
\]
Answer: 2:3

Q15. A machine prints 100 pages per hour. It runs for 8 hours and 85% pages are usable. How many usable pages are obtained?
A. 686 pages
B. 680 pages
C. 544 pages
D. 679 pages
Answer: 680 pages
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_work_quality_rejection
Explanation:
Convert each person's work into contribution units.
\[
O_a=O\times \frac{p}{100}
\]
Use contribution ratio for the share.
\[
O_a=100\times 8\times \frac{85}{100}
\]
Apply the wage or contract amount.
\[
O_a=680
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
O_a=gross\times p/100
\]
Answer: 680 pages

Q19. A filling pipe fills a tank in 12 hours and a leak empties it in 36 hours. How long will it take to fill the tank when both work together?
A. 17 hours
B. 27 hours
C. 18 hours
D. 22 hours
Answer: 18 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_basic_fill_empty
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
T=\frac{W_l}{r_f-r_e}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
r=1/12-1/36
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
T=18
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W_l}{r_n}
\]
Answer: 18 hours

Q20. A pipe fills a tank in 15 hours. A leak slows the filling to 20 hours. What time will the leak take to empty the tank alone?
A. 60 hours
B. 56 hours
C. 90 hours
D. 58 hours
Answer: 60 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{F}-\frac{1}{T}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{15}-\frac{1}{20}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=60
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 60 hours

Q21. The working capacity of A is to B as 3:4. If both finish the job in 12 days, how many days will A take alone?
A. 28 days
B. 16 days
C. 19 days
D. 9 days
Answer: 28 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_relative_efficiency_percent
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
W=(a+b)T
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
W=(3+4)\times 12
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_A=\frac{84}{3}=28
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T_A=\frac{(a+b)T}{a}=\frac{(3+4)12}{3}=28
\]
Answer: 28 days

---

Q22. If each man completes 4 items daily and each woman completes 3, how long will 6 men and 8 women take for 240 items?

T=\frac{240}{6\times 4+8\times 3}
\]

\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:

\]
Answer: 5 days

Q23. There are 720 ration packets in a camp. If 36 packets are used each day, how many days will they last?
A. 30 days
B. 24 days
C. 17 days
D. 20 days
Answer: 20 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_resource_rate_equivalence
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
D=\frac{S}{R}
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
D=\frac{720}{36}
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D=20
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=\frac{S}{R}
\]
Answer: 20 days

Q24. Food is sufficient for 24 people for 37 1/2 days. After 6 days, the number of people becomes 29. For how many more days will the food last?
A. 30.07 days
B. 26.07 days
C. 20.86 days
D. 24.07 days
Answer: 26.07 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_food_population_change
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
D=\frac{S-consumed}{people}
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
D=\frac{900-24\times 6}{29}
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D=26.07
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=\frac{S_l}{P_2}
\]
Answer: 26.07 days

Q26. A and B can separately complete a work in 20 and 8 days. A is absent for the last 2 days and B is absent for the last 3 days. In how many days is the work completed?
A. 6.43 days
B. 8.43 days
C. 6.32 days
D. 3.43 days
Answer: 8.43 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_backward_leave
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r_A(T-a)+r_B(T-b)=W
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
14(T-2)+35(T-3)=280
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=8.43
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 8.43 days

---

Q27. A job is finished by 18 workers in 8 days when each works 5 hours daily. How many workers are needed for 12 days at 6 hours daily?
A. 10 workers
B. 18 workers
C. 27 workers
D. 13 workers
Answer: 10 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_road_construction_work
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
18\times 8\times 5=M_2\times 12\times 6
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
M_2=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{18\times 8\times 5}{12\times 6}=10
\]
Answer: 10 workers

---

Q29. For 2 days a team completes 15 forms per day. Then the team changes and completes 26 forms per day. How many days will 120 forms take altogether?

B. 4.37 days
C. 5.46 days
D. 4.46 days
Answer: 5.46 days

Family: tw_multi_phase_join_leave

\[
\sum r_it_i=W
\]

\[
W_1=15\times 2
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[

\]

\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]

Q30. The job takes 10 days when A and B work together. If A alone takes 12 days, how long will B alone take?

Q32. A and B can separately complete a job in 18 days and 10 days. What is the time taken when they work together?
A. 14 days
B. 28 days
C. 6.43 days
D. 10 days
Answer: 6.43 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(18,10)=90
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=90/18=5,\quad B=90/10=9
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{90}{5+9}=6.43
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{18\times 10}{18+10}=6.43
\]
Answer: 6.43 days

Q33. The full team A+B+C finishes a job in 10 days. The known worker time is 15 days. How many days will the remaining worker take alone?

\[

\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T_X=1/r_X
\]
Answer: 30 days

Q38. A contractor receives ₹8000 and a bonus of ₹150 per day saved. For 3 days saved, what is the total amount?
A. ₹8447
B. ₹8455
C. ₹8450
D. ₹6337.5
Answer: ₹8450
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_contract_penalty_bonus
Explanation:
Convert each person's work into contribution units.
\[
E=base+bonus
\]
Use contribution ratio for the share.
\[
E=8000+3\times 150
\]
Apply the wage or contract amount.
\[
E=8450
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[

Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=30/10=3,\quad B=30/15=2
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{30}{3+2}=6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{10\times 15}{10+15}=6
\]
Answer: 6 days

---

Q112. A filling pipe fills a tank in 12 hours and a leak empties it in 36 hours. How long will it take to fill the tank when both work together?

B. 9 hours

D. 12 hours
Answer: 10 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time

\[

\[

\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
B=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 10 hours

Q113. Two workers take turns, with A starting. A handles 6 files per turn and B handles 4 files per turn. In how many days will 120 files be finished?
A. 25 days
B. 24 days
C. 36 days
D. 20 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_terminal_overshoot_cycle
Explanation:
Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[
W_{cycle}=6+4
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 24 days

Q114. A can complete a piece of work in 18 days and B can complete the same work in 6 days. In how many days will they complete it working together?
A. 24 days
B. 4 1/2 days
C. 12 days
D. 6 days
Answer: 4 1/2 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(18,6)=18
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=18/18=1,\quad B=18/6=3
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{18}{1+3}=4.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{18\times 6}{18+6}=4.5
\]
Answer: 4 1/2 days

Q115. Three workers receive ₹12000 for a job. Their contributions are proportional to 8:12. How much will A get?

C. ₹4805
D. ₹5760
Answer: ₹4800
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_wage_partial_time
Explanation:
Convert each person's work into contribution units.
\[
Share=\frac{c_i}{\sum c}\times W
\]
Use contribution ratio for the share.
\[
Share=\frac{8}{20}\times 12000
\]
Apply the wage or contract amount.
\[
Share=4800
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
Share=contribution\ ratio\times wage
\]
Answer: ₹4800

Q126. A pipe can fill a tank in 10 hours. Due to a leak, the tank is filled in 16 hours. In how many hours can the leak empty the full tank?

B. 26 2/3 hours
C. 27 2/3 hours
D. 31 2/3 hours
Answer: 26 2/3 hours
Difficulty: Medium

\[

\[

\]

\[

\]

\[

\]

\]

Q133. A work requires 15 workers for 16 days with 6 working hours per day. How many workers will be needed for 12 days with 8 hours per day?

Family: tw_road_construction_work
Explanation:

\[

\]

\[

\]

\[
M_2=15
\]

\[
M_2=\frac{15\times 16\times 6}{12\times 8}=15
\]
Answer: 15 workers

Q150. If A can complete a work in 6 days and B can complete it in 30 days, how many days are needed when both work at the same time?

Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:

\[

\[
A=30/6=5,\quad B=30/30=1
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{30}{5+1}=5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{6\times 30}{6+30}=5
\]
Answer: 5 days

Q162. A filling pipe takes 10 hours for a tank. With a leak, the same tank fills in 20 hours. In how many hours can the leak empty a full tank?
A. 20 hours
B. 17 hours
C. 15 hours
D. 25 hours
Answer: 20 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:

\[

\]

\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{10}-\frac{1}{20}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=20
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 20 hours

\]

\]
Answer: 19 1/5 days

Q168. In a joint job, A and B have individual times 18 and 6 days. A is not present in the last 2 days and B in the last 3 days. What is the completion time?

\]

\]
Answer: 7 1/4 days

Q169. 12 workers can build a road stretch in 10 days by working 8 hours a day. How many workers are needed to finish it in 16 days by working 6 hours a day?

D. 12 workers
Answer: 10 workers

Family: tw_road_construction_work
Explanation:

\[

\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
12\times 10\times 8=M_2\times 16\times 6
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
M_2=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{12\times 10\times 8}{16\times 6}=10
\]
Answer: 10 workers

\]

B_c
\]

Q201. A pipe fills a tank in 12 hours. A leak slows the filling to 18 hours. What time will the leak take to empty the tank alone?
A. 34 hours
B. 36 hours
C. 28 4/5 hours
D. 40 hours
Answer: 36 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{F}-\frac{1}{T}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{12}-\frac{1}{18}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=36
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 36 hours

Family: tw_delayed_join
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W_1=r_At_1,\ T_2=\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=28\times 4,\quad R=840-112
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=4+\frac{728}{28+105}=9.47
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=4+\frac{840-28\times 4}{28+105}
\]
Answer: 9.47 days

Q240. One worker can do a job in 30 days and another can do it in 24 days. How long will they take together?

Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work

\[

\]

\[

\]

\[
T=\frac{120}{4+5}=13\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{30\times 24}{30+24}=13\frac{1}{3}
\]
Answer: 13 1/3 days

\[
N_{extra}=14
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 14 workers

Q263. Food for 30 people lasts 24 days. If after 6 days the number of people changes to 35, how many more days will it last?
A. 11.57 days
B. 20.43 days
C. 17.43 days
D. 15.43 days
Answer: 15.43 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_food_remaining_stock
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
D=\frac{S-consumed}{people}
\]

\[
D=\frac{720-30\times 6}{35}
\]

\[
D=15.43
\]

\[

\]
Answer: 15.43 days

Q264. A filling pipe fills a tank in 12 hours and a leak empties it in 36 hours. How long will it take to fill the tank when both work together?
A. 57 hours
B. 60 hours
C. 66 hours
D. 48 hours
Answer: 60 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{10}-\frac{1}{12}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
B=60
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 60 hours

Q266. A can complete a piece of work in 6 days and B can complete the same work in 15 days. In how many days will they complete it working together?
A. 4.29 days
B. 10 1/2 days
C. 21 days
D. 6 days
Answer: 4.29 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(6,15)=30
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=30/6=5,\quad B=30/15=2
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{30}{5+2}=4.29
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{6\times 15}{6+15}=4.29
\]
Answer: 4.29 days

Q274. A alone takes 24 days and B alone takes 6 days. During a joint job, A does not work in the last 2 days and B does not work in the last 3 days. What is the total time?

D. 6 3/5 days
Answer: 7 3/5 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_backward_leave
Explanation:

\[
r_A(T-a)+r_B(T-b)=W
\]

\[
5(T-2)+20(T-3)=120
\]

T=7.6

\]

Q275. 10 workers can build a road stretch in 15 days by working 8 hours a day. How many workers are needed to finish it in 20 days by working 6 hours a day?

Answer: 10 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_road_construction_work
Explanation:

\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
10\times 15\times 8=M_2\times 20\times 6
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
M_2=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{10\times 15\times 8}{20\times 6}=10
\]
Answer: 10 workers

Explanation:

\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]

\[

\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=22.25
\]

\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 22 1/4 days

Q300. A alone finishes a job in 8 days, while B alone finishes it in 24 days. How many days will the job take if both work together?
A. 16 days
B. 32 days
C. 6 days
D. 8 days
Answer: 6 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(8,24)=24
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=24/8=3,\quad B=24/24=1
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{24}{3+1}=6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{8\times 24}{8+24}=6
\]
Answer: 6 days

B=8.57

\]

B. 6 1/2 days
C. 9.38 days
D. 2 1/2 days
Answer: 7 1/2 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_phase_state_reconstruction
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
x=\frac{W-W_1}{r}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
x=\frac{120-60}{8}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
x=7.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
x=\frac{W_l}{r}
\]
Answer: 7 1/2 days

---

Q337. A and B work on alternate hours, starting with A. A completes 6 files in a hour and B completes 4 files in a hour. If 120 files are to be completed, in how many hours will the job be finished?
A. 24 hours
B. 30 hours
C. 12 hours
D. 27 hours
Answer: 24 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_alternating_hours
Explanation:
Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[
W_{cycle}=6+4
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 24 hours

---

Q338. A does \(\frac{4}{9}\) of a job in 9 days. What is the time required for the complete job?
A. 18 days
B. 20 1/4 days
C. 4 days
D. 81 days
Answer: 20 1/4 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_partial_completion_then_team
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
T=d\times \frac{q}{p}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
T=9\times \frac{9}{4}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=20.25
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=9\div \frac{4}{9}=20.25
\]
Answer: 20 1/4 days

Q339. A job has 630 files. The team handles 77 files daily at first for 5 days, and then 65 files daily. What is the total time?
A. 10.52 days
B. 8.77 days
C. 13.77 days
D. 6.77 days
Answer: 8.77 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_replacement_worker
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
r_1t_1+r_2t_2=W
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=77\times 5
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=5+\frac{245}{65}=8.77
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 8.77 days

---

Q340. A group is expected to complete a work in 18 days. It has already worked for 5 days. What fraction remains?
A. 18/13
B. 5/18
C. 13/18
D. 13/5
Answer: 13/18
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_residual_work
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
R=1-\frac{t}{T}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
R=1-\frac{5}{18}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
R=\frac{13}{18}=13/18
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{18-5}{18}=13/18
\]
Answer: 13/18

Q341. 8 equal workers begin a job. After 10 days, only 5 workers remain. By how many days is the work delayed?

C. 15 1/2 days
D. 16 1/2 days
Answer: 16 1/2 days

Family: tw_worker_removed_delay

\[
D=T_{actual}-T_{planned}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
R=300-8\times 10
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
D=\frac{220}{5}-\frac{220}{8}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=T_a-T_p
\]
Answer: 16 1/2 days

Q342. A job needs 7 1/2 days of work. Work stops for 1 days during the job. How many days pass from start to finish?
A. 3 1/2 days
B. 10.63 days
C. 8 1/2 days
D. 5 1/2 days
Answer: 8 1/2 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_interrupted_work
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
T_{calendar}=T_{active}+T_{idle}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
T_{active}=\frac{120}{12+4}
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=T_{active}+1=8.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_a+t_i
\]
Answer: 8 1/2 days

Q343. A and B jointly work for 4 days. A then leaves, and B completes the job. If A and B alone need 30 and 8 days, what is the total time?
A. 2.93 days
B. 8.32 days
C. 5.93 days
D. 6.93 days
Answer: 6.93 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_forward_leave
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W=(r_A+r_B)t_1+r_Bt_2
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=(4+15)\times 4
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=4+\frac{44}{15}=6.93
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=4+\frac{W-W_1}{B}
\]
Answer: 6.93 days

Q344. 10 workers have done 80 files out of 240. To finish the rest in 8 days, how many additional workers are needed?
A. 8 workers
B. 10 workers
C. 6 workers
D. 15 workers
Answer: 10 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-N_c
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{160}{1\times 8}\right\rceil-10
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 10 workers

Q345. Food for 20 people lasts 30 days. If after 8 days the number of people changes to 25, how many more days will it last?
A. 22 3/5 days
B. 17 3/5 days
C. 16 3/5 days
D. 13 1/5 days
Answer: 17 3/5 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_food_remaining_stock
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
D=\frac{S-consumed}{people}
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
D=\frac{600-20\times 8}{25}
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D=17.6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=\frac{S_l}{P_2}
\]
Answer: 17 3/5 days

Q346. A tank is filled by a pipe taking 12 hours and emptied by another pipe taking 36 hours. In how many hours will the tank be filled if both are opened?
A. 15 1/3 hours
B. 16 2/3 hours
C. 8 1/3 hours
D. 13 1/3 hours
Answer: 13 1/3 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{8}-\frac{1}{20}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
B=13\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 13 1/3 hours

---

Q348. A alone finishes a job in 30 days, while B alone finishes it in 8 days. How many days will the job take if both work together?
A. 38 days
B. 19 days
C. 6.32 days
D. 8 days
Answer: 6.32 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(30,8)=120
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=120/30=4,\quad B=120/8=15
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{120}{4+15}=6.32
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{30\times 8}{30+8}=6.32
\]
Answer: 6.32 days

---

Q351. A printing job produces 80 pages each hour for 8 hours. With 90% acceptance, what is the accepted page count?

B. 460.8 pages
C. 576 pages
D. 575 pages
Answer: 576 pages

Explanation:

\[

\]

\[
O_a=80\times 8\times \frac{90}{100}
\]
Apply the wage or contract amount.
\[
O_a=576
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
O_a=gross\times p/100
\]
Answer: 576 pages

Q353. A pipe can fill a tank in 12 hours. Due to a leak, the tank is filled in 17 hours. In how many hours can the leak empty the full tank?
A. 40 4/5 hours
B. 44 4/5 hours
C. 41 4/5 hours
D. 32.64 hours
Answer: 40 4/5 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{F}-\frac{1}{T}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{12}-\frac{1}{17}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=40.8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 40 4/5 hours

Q355. A and B work on a job but leave before it is completed. A leaves 2 days early and B leaves 3 days early. If their individual times are 6 and 15 days, how long does the job take?
A. 10.57 days
B. 5.26 days
C. 6.57 days
D. 7.57 days
Answer: 6.57 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_backward_leave
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r_A(T-a)+r_B(T-b)=W
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
10(T-2)+4(T-3)=60
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=6.57
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 6.57 days

Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=7\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 7 1/3 days

\]

Q375. A pipe fills a tank in 15 hours. A leak slows the filling to 21 hours. What time will the leak take to empty the tank alone?
A. 65.62 hours
B. 55 1/2 hours
C. 58 1/2 hours
D. 52 1/2 hours
Answer: 52 1/2 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{F}-\frac{1}{T}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{15}-\frac{1}{21}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=52.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 52 1/2 hours

Q377. In a joint job, A and B have individual times 12 and 18 days. A is not present in the last 2 days and B in the last 3 days. What is the completion time?
A. 9 3/5 days
B. 14 3/5 days
C. 10 3/5 days
D. 11.52 days
Answer: 9 3/5 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_backward_leave
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r_A(T-a)+r_B(T-b)=W
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
3(T-2)+2(T-3)=36
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=9.6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 9 3/5 days

Q381. One worker can do a job in 12 days and another can do it in 24 days. How long will they take together?
A. 36 days
B. 18 days
C. 8 days
D. 12 days
Answer: 8 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(12,24)=24
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=24/12=2,\quad B=24/24=1
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{24}{2+1}=8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{12\times 24}{12+24}=8
\]
Answer: 8 days

C. 1/3
D. 3/2
Answer: 2/3
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_residual_work
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
R=1-\frac{t}{T}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
R=1-\frac{10}{30}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
R=\frac{20}{30}=2/3
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{30-10}{30}=2/3
\]
Answer: 2/3

\]

Q413. A and B start a work together. A can complete it in 18 days and B in 6 days. After 3 days, A leaves. In how many days from the start will the work be completed?

\]
Answer: 5 days

Q416. Pipe A is an inlet of 12 hours and pipe B is an outlet of 36 hours. If both are opened together, how many hours will the tank take to fill?
A. 22.14 hours
B. 17.14 hours
C. 19.14 hours
D. 12.86 hours
Answer: 17.14 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{8}-\frac{1}{15}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
B=17.14
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 17.14 hours

Q418. A takes 24 days to finish a work and B takes 15 days for the same work. In how many days can A and B finish it together?

\]

\[

\]
Answer: 9.23 days

C. 12 days

\[

\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[

\]

T=12
\]

\[

\]
Answer: 12 days

\]

Q484. A team processes 6 files per day for the first 3 days. After that, more workers join and the team processes 41 files per day. If 120 files must be completed, how many days are needed in all?
A. 4.39 days
B. 5.49 days
C. 6.49 days
D. 9.49 days
Answer: 5.49 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_multi_phase_join_leave
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
\sum r_it_i=W
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=6\times 3
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=3+\frac{102}{41}=5.49
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 5.49 days

Q485. A and B together finish a job in 6 days. A's individual time is 12 days. What is B's individual time?
A. 18 days
B. 9 days
C. 12 days
D. 6 days
Answer: 12 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_individual_from_combined
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{6}-\frac{1}{12}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
B=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{6\times 12}{12-6}=12
\]
Answer: 12 days

Q490. If 30 people can use a food stock for 24 days, how long will the stock last for 30 people?
A. 18 days
B. 19 days
C. 25 days
D. 24 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_food_resource_basic
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
M_1D_1=M_2D_2
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
30\times 24=30\times D_2
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D_2=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D_2=\frac{30\times 24}{30}
\]
Answer: 24 days

Q492. 10 workers have completed 100 of 360 forms. Only 12 days are left. How many extra workers are needed to finish on time?

D. 13 workers
Answer: 12 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_worker_added_to_meet_deadline
Explanation:

\[

\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[

\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 12 workers

Q498. One worker can do a job in 30 days and another can do it in 12 days. How long will they take together?
A. 8.57 days
B. 42 days
C. 21 days
D. 12 days
Answer: 8.57 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(30,12)=60
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=60/30=2,\quad B=60/12=5
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{60}{2+5}=8.57
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{30\times 12}{30+12}=8.57
\]
Answer: 8.57 days
